<script>
    function initJivochat()
    {
        var widget_id = 'YXjTTjlebo';
        var s = document.createElement('script'); s.type = 'text/javascript'; s.async = true;s.src = '//code.jivosite.com/script/geo-widget/'+widget_id; var ss = document.getElementsByTagName('script')[0]; ss.parentNode.insertBefore(s, ss);
    }

    function getVisitorCookie(name)
    {
        var matches = document.cookie.match(new RegExp("(?:^|; )" + name.replace(/([\.$?*|{}\(\)\[\]\\\/\+^])/g, '\\$1') + "=([^;]*)"));
        return matches ? decodeURIComponent(matches[1]) : undefined
    }

    function jivo_onOpen()
    {
        var visitor_uid = getVisitorCookie("isvisitor");
        visitor_uid = (visitor_uid.indexOf('.') != -1) ? visitor_uid.substring(0,visitor_uid.indexOf('.')) : visitor_uid;
        jivo_api.setUserToken(visitor_uid);
    }

    function jivo_startCall(phone)
    {
        jivo_api.startCall(phone);
    }

    initJivochat();
</script>
